function uep08258AdmissionCompare(a,b){const ga=uep08258AdmissionRank(a),gb=uep08258AdmissionRank(b);if(ga!==gb)return ga-gb;return dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b)||String(a?.['전형명']||'').localeCompare(String(b?.['전형명']||''),'ko');}
function uep08261OfficialMajor(row){return String(row?.['대전형']||'').trim();}
function uep08261AdmissionGroup(row){const d=uep08261OfficialMajor(row);if(d==='학생부교과')return '교과';if(d==='학생부종합')return '종합';if(d==='논술')return '논술';if(d==='실기/실적')return '실기';if(d==='정시 수능'||d==='정시 실기'||d.startsWith('정시 '))return '정시';return '기타';}
function uep08261RegionalDoctor(row){const name=String(row?.['전형명']||'').trim();return name.includes('지역의사');}
function uep08261AdmissionCardClass(row){const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(row);if(i.unavailable)return ' uep-admission-unavailable-card';if(uep08261RegionalDoctor(row))return ' uep-admission-regional-doctor';if(i.restricted)return ' uep-admission-restricted-card';const group=uep08261AdmissionGroup(row);if(group==='교과')return ' uep-admission-subject';if(group==='종합')return ' uep-admission-holistic';if(group==='논술')return ' uep-admission-essay';if(group==='정시')return ' uep-admission-regular';return ' uep-admission-other';}
function uep08258AdmissionSupportBadge(row){const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(row);if(i.unavailable)return '<span class="uep-admission-unho-unavailable">운호고 지원대상 아님</span>';if(i.restricted)return '<span class="uep-admission-restricted-badge">지원자격 확인</span>';return '';}

function dashboardStudentStatusCompactMarkup(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today),yesterday=new Date(today);yesterday.setDate(today.getDate()-1);const yesterdayKey=dateKey(yesterday);
  const official=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(todayKey));
  const late=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(row=>uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day)===todayKey));
  const night=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||'').slice(0,10)===yesterdayKey&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row)));
  const currentStudents=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(readonlyCache?.students||[]),u=dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity();
  const cards=[['status','late','공결/지각',`공결 ${new Set(official.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size} · 지각 ${new Set(late.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}`],['status','night','야자출결',`${new Set(night.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}/${currentStudents.length||259}`],['admission','basics','대입 이해','기초부터 실제 전형까지'],['admission','major','전공별 대학 찾기','전공·지역·전형·최저 검색'],['admission','university','오늘의 대학',u?.['대학명']||'대학 정보']];
  return `<div class="uep-student-status-compact uep-dashboard-five-cards">${cards.map(([type,key,label,value])=>`<button class="${type==='admission'?'dashboard-admission-card':''}" ${type==='status'?`data-dashboard-student-status="${key}"`:`data-dashboard-admission="${key}"`}><span>${label}</span><b>${escapeHtml(value)}</b></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function dashboardStudentStatusMarkup(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const yesterday=new Date(today);yesterday.setDate(today.getDate()-1);const yesterdayKey=dateKey(yesterday);
  const official=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(todayKey));
  const late=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(row=>uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day)===todayKey));
  const night=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||'').slice(0,10)===yesterdayKey&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row)));
  const reportRows=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(dashboardReportStatusRows());
  const targetGroups=dashboardReportGroups(reportRows);
  const submitted=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="complete");
  const progress=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="progress");
  const missing=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="missing");
  const scope=dashboardGradeScope();
  const currentStudents=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(readonlyCache?.students||[]);
  const officialCount=new Set(official.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const lateCount=new Set(late.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const nightCount=new Set(night.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const nightTotal=currentStudents.length||259;
  const metrics=[
    ['late','공결/지각',`${officialCount}/${lateCount}`,'attendance',`공결 ${officialCount}명 · 지각 ${lateCount}명`,'명'],
    ['night','야자출결',`${nightCount}/${nightTotal}`,'attendance','야자 출결 학생','명'],
    ['submitted','제출완료',submitted.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 제출완료 학생','명'],
    ['progress','제출중',progress.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 일부 제출 학생','명'],
    ['report','미제출',missing.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 미제출 학생','명']
  ];
  return `<div class="uep-student-scope"><span>조회 기준 · <b>${escapeHtml(scope.label)}</b></span><strong>선택활동 대상 <b>${targetGroups.length}</b>명</strong><em>완료 ${submitted.length}명 · 제출중 ${progress.length}명 · 미제출 ${missing.length}명</em></div><div class="uep-student-kpis">${metrics.map(([kind,label,count,page,desc,unit])=>`<button class="kpi-${kind}" data-dashboard-student-status="${kind}" data-page="${page}" title="${desc} 조회"><span>${label}</span><b>${count}</b><small>${kind==='late'?desc:unit}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* UEP_08218_CHANGELOG_POPUP */
(function uep08218ReleaseNotes(){
  const VERSION='0.82.18';
  const STORAGE_KEY='uep:release-notes:'+VERSION;
  function show(){
    try{if(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)==='shown')return;}catch{}
    if(document.querySelector('[data-uep-release-notes="'+VERSION+'"]'))return;
    const layer=document.createElement('div');
    layer.className='uep-release-notes-layer';
    layer.dataset.uepReleaseNotes=VERSION;
    layer.innerHTML='<section class="uep-release-notes-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="uep-release-notes-title"><header><div><small>UEP UPDATE</small><h2 id="uep-release-notes-title">v0.82.18 복구 업데이트</h2></div><button type="button" aria-label="닫기" data-uep-release-notes-close>×</button></header><div class="uep-release-notes-body"><article><b>구글시트 연결 복구 기준판</b><p>실시간 구글시트 연결이 정상 동작하던 v0.82.14 패키지를 기준으로 다시 구성했습니다.</p></article><article><b>공결·지각 상단 정리</b><p>학교 공결대장 · 기준일 · 학생 수 · 조회 범위 4개 카드를 한 줄로 배치했습니다.</p></article><article><b>업데이트 수정내역 1회 안내</b><p>새 버전 설치 후 최초 실행 시에만 이 안내가 표시됩니다.</p></article><article><b>대입 화면 기본구조 유지</b><p>v0.82.14에서 정상 동작하던 대입 기초·전형 이해·오늘의 대학 구조는 그대로 유지합니다.</p></article></div><footer><button type="button" data-uep-release-notes-confirm>확인</button></footer></section>';
    const close=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY,'shown');}catch{}layer.remove();};
    layer.querySelector('[data-uep-release-notes-close]')?.addEventListener('click',close);
    layer.querySelector('[data-uep-release-notes-confirm]')?.addEventListener('click',close);
    layer.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();});
    document.body.appendChild(layer);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(show,350),{once:true});
  else setTimeout(show,350);
})();

function openDashboardAdmissionDialog(title,html){
  const isUniversity=String(html||'').includes('주요 전형과 선발방식');
  if(isUniversity){
    const origin=window.__uepAdmissionReturn||'today';
    const label=origin==='types'?'전형 이해로':'오늘의 대학으로';
    const back=`<div class="uep-admission-backbar"><button type="button" class="uep-admission-back" data-uep-admission-back="${origin}">← ${label}</button></div>`;

  }
  const result=openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase(title,html);
  setTimeout(()=>{uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog();document.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-admission-back]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const o=btn.dataset.uepAdmissionBack;window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';if(o==='types')openDashboardAdmissionTypes();else if(typeof openDashboardTodayUniversity==='function')openDashboardTodayUniversity();else if(typeof openDashboardUniversity==='function')openDashboardUniversity();});},20);
  return result;
}

function openDashboardAdmissionDialog(title,html){
  const isUniversity=String(html||'').includes('주요 전형과 선발방식');
  if(isUniversity){
    const origin=window.__uepAdmissionReturn||'today';
    const label=origin==='types'?'전형 이해로':'오늘의 대학으로';
    const back=`<div class="uep-admission-backbar"><button type="button" class="uep-admission-back" data-uep-admission-back="${origin}">← ${label}</button></div>`;
    html=back+`<div class="uep-admission-university-detail">${html}</div>`;
  }
  const result=openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase(title,html);
  setTimeout(()=>{uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog();document.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-admission-back]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const o=btn.dataset.uepAdmissionBack;window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';if(o==='types')openDashboardAdmissionTypes();else if(typeof openDashboardTodayUniversity==='function')openDashboardTodayUniversity();else if(typeof openDashboardUniversity==='function')openDashboardUniversity();});},20);
  return result;
}

/* UEP_08224_ADMISSION_STATE_RESET */
(function(){
  const originalBase=openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase;
  openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase=function(title,body){
    originalBase(title,body);
    const layer=document.querySelector('.dashboard-admission-layer');
    const closeBtn=layer?.querySelector('[data-admission-close]');
    const reset=()=>{window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';window.__uepAdmissionRegion='';};
    if(closeBtn){const old=closeBtn.onclick;closeBtn.onclick=(e)=>{reset();if(typeof old==='function')old.call(closeBtn,e);};}
    if(layer){const oldLayer=layer.onclick;layer.onclick=(e)=>{if(e.target===layer)reset();if(typeof oldLayer==='function')oldLayer.call(layer,e);};}
  };
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const btn=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('[data-dashboard-admission]'):null;
    if(!btn)return;
    const key=btn.getAttribute('data-dashboard-admission');
    if(key==='university'){window.__uepAdmissionReturn='today';window.__uepAdmissionRegion='';}
    else if(key==='types'||key==='basics'){window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';window.__uepAdmissionRegion='';}
  },true);
})();

function openDashboardAdmissionBasics(){
  window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';window.__uepAdmissionRegion='';
  const rows=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionBasics','52_대입기초').filter(dashboardAdmissionEnabled).sort((a,b)=>dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b));
  if(!rows.length)return openDashboardAdmissionDialog('대입 기초','<p>52_대입기초 자료를 읽지 못했습니다.</p>');
  const groups=[];
  for(const row of rows){const key=String(row['대분류']||'대입 기초').trim()||'대입 기초';let group=groups.find(x=>x.key===key);if(!group){group={key,rows:[]};groups.push(group);}group.rows.push(row);}
  const html=groups.map(group=>'<section><h3>'+escapeHtml(group.key)+'</h3><div class="admission-explain-grid">'+group.rows.map(row=>'<article class="uep-basics-card"><b>'+escapeHtml(row['주제']||'')+'</b><p>'+escapeHtml(row['카드요약']||row['상세설명']||'')+'</p>'+(row['상세설명']&&row['상세설명']!==row['카드요약']?'<small>'+escapeHtml(row['상세설명'])+'</small>':'')+(row['상담포인트']?'<small><strong>상담</strong> · '+escapeHtml(row['상담포인트'])+'</small>':'')+(row['주의/오해']?'<small><strong>주의</strong> · '+escapeHtml(row['주의/오해'])+'</small>':'')+'</article>').join('')+'</div></section>').join('');
  openDashboardAdmissionDialog('대입 기초','<div class="admission-learning-flow uep-basics-flow">'+html+'<p class="admission-reference-note">52_대입기초 실시간 연결 · 2029 대입 기준, 대학별 세부사항은 최종 모집요강 재확인</p></div>');
}

function officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(date){
  // __UEP_08162_OFFICIAL_SINGLE_DATE_SOURCE__
  const key=String(date||'').slice(0,10);
  return (readonlyCache?.officialAttendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row?.date||'').slice(0,10)===key);
}
function dashboardAdmissionRows(...keys){
  for(const key of keys){const rows=readonlyCache?.[key];if(Array.isArray(rows))return rows;}
  return [];
}
function dashboardAdmissionEnabled(row){return String(row?.['사용여부']??row?.use??'Y').toUpperCase()!=='N';}
function dashboardAdmissionOrder(row){return Number(row?.['노출순서']??row?.order??9999)||9999;}
function dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(value){return String(value||'').replace(/대학교/g,'대').replace(/\s|\(.*?\)/g,'');}
function dashboardAdmissionTodayIndex(length){if(!length)return 0;const d=new Date(),s=new Date(d.getFullYear(),0,0);return Math.floor((d-s)/86400000)%length;}
function dashboardAdmissionUniversities(){return dashboardAdmissionRows('universityAdmissions','56_대학입시마스터').filter(dashboardAdmissionEnabled).sort((a,b)=>dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b));}
function dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity(){const rows=dashboardAdmissionUniversities();return rows[dashboardAdmissionTodayIndex(rows.length)]||null;}
function openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase(title,body){
  document.querySelector('.dashboard-admission-layer')?.remove();
  const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.className='dashboard-admission-layer counsel-reason-layer';
  layer.innerHTML='<div class="dashboard-admission-dialog"><header><div><small>UEP 대입상담 · 교육용 참고</small><h2>'+escapeHtml(title)+'</h2></div><button type="button" data-admission-close>×</button></header><div class="dashboard-admission-body">'+body+'</div></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(layer);const close=()=>layer.remove();layer.querySelector('[data-admission-close]').onclick=close;layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();};
}
function openDashboardAdmissionBasics(){
  const rows=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionBasics','52_대입기초').filter(dashboardAdmissionEnabled).sort((a,b)=>dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b));
  if(!rows.length)return openDashboardAdmissionDialog('대입 기초','<p>52_대입기초 자료를 읽지 못했습니다.</p>');
  const groups=[];
  for(const row of rows){const key=String(row['대분류']||'대입 기초').trim()||'대입 기초';let group=groups.find(x=>x.key===key);if(!group){group={key,rows:[]};groups.push(group);}group.rows.push(row);}
  const html=groups.map(group=>`<section><h3>${escapeHtml(group.key)}</h3><div class="admission-explain-grid">${group.rows.map(row=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(row['주제']||'')}</b><p>${escapeHtml(row['카드요약']||row['상세설명']||'')}</p>${row['상세설명']&&row['상세설명']!==row['카드요약']?`<small>${escapeHtml(row['상세설명'])}</small>`:''}${row['상담포인트']?`<small><strong>상담</strong> · ${escapeHtml(row['상담포인트'])}</small>`:''}${row['주의/오해']?`<small><strong>주의</strong> · ${escapeHtml(row['주의/오해'])}</small>`:''}</article>`).join('')}</div></section>`).join('');
  openDashboardAdmissionDialog('대입 기초',`<div class="admission-learning-flow">${html}<p class="admission-reference-note">52_대입기초 실시간 연결 · 2029 대입 기준, 대학별 세부사항은 최종 모집요강 재확인</p></div>`);
}
function dashboardAdmissionMethod(row){
  const text=[row['선발방식'],row['평가구조요약'],row['핵심평가'],row['주요평가자료'],row['한줄요약'],row['상담표시문구']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  if(row['선발방식'])return String(row['선발방식']);if(row['평가구조요약'])return String(row['평가구조요약']);
  const out=[];if(/교과|내신/.test(text))out.push('내신');if(/수능최저|최저/.test(text))out.push('수능최저');if(/서류|학생부/.test(text))out.push('서류');if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');if(/논술/.test(text))out.unshift('논술');if(/수능/.test(text)&&/정시|수능/.test(String(row['전형유형']||'')))return '수능100 또는 영역별 반영';return [...new Set(out)].join(' + ')||'대학별 방식 확인';
}

function uep08258AdmissionCompare(a,b){const ga=uep08258AdmissionRank(a),gb=uep08258AdmissionRank(b);if(ga!==gb)return ga-gb;return dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b)||String(a?.['전형명']||'').localeCompare(String(b?.['전형명']||''),'ko');}
function uep08261OfficialMajor(row){return String(row?.['대전형']||'').trim();}
function uep08261AdmissionGroup(row){const d=uep08261OfficialMajor(row);if(d==='학생부교과')return '교과';if(d==='학생부종합')return '종합';if(d==='논술')return '논술';if(d==='실기/실적')return '실기';if(d==='정시 수능'||d==='정시 실기'||d.startsWith('정시 '))return '정시';return '기타';}
function uep08261RegionalDoctor(row){const name=String(row?.['전형명']||'').trim();return name.includes('지역의사');}
function uep08261AdmissionCardClass(row){const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(row);if(i.unavailable)return ' uep-admission-unavailable-card';if(uep08261RegionalDoctor(row))return ' uep-admission-regional-doctor';if(i.restricted)return ' uep-admission-restricted-card';const group=uep08261AdmissionGroup(row);if(group==='교과')return ' uep-admission-subject';if(group==='종합')return ' uep-admission-holistic';if(group==='논술')return ' uep-admission-essay';if(group==='정시')return ' uep-admission-regular';return ' uep-admission-other';}
function uep08258AdmissionSupportBadge(row){const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(row);if(i.unavailable)return '<span class="uep-admission-unho-unavailable">운호고 지원대상 아님</span>';if(i.restricted)return '<span class="uep-admission-restricted-badge">지원자격 확인</span>';return '';}
function openDashboardUniversityDetail(university=dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity()){
  if(!university)return openDashboardAdmissionDialog('오늘의 대학','<p>56_대학입시마스터 자료를 읽지 못했습니다.</p>');
  const name=university['대학명']||'오늘의 대학',displayName=uep08251UniversityDisplayNameForRegion(university,window.__uepAdmissionRegion)||name,norm=dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(name);
  const structures=dashboardAdmissionStructureRows().filter(r=>uep08258AdmissionActive(r)&&dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm);
  const minimums=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionMinimumRows','54_수능최저DB').filter(r=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm);
  const results=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionResults','55_대학입결DB').filter(r=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm);
  const admissions=[...(structures.length?structures:minimums)].sort(uep08258AdmissionCompare);
  const origin=['types','guide','major'].includes(window.__uepAdmissionReturn)?window.__uepAdmissionReturn:'today';
  const nav=origin==='types'||origin==='guide'?'<div class="uep-uni-type-back"><button type="button" data-uep-back-types>← 대입 이해로</button></div>':origin==='major'?'<div class="uep-uni-type-back"><button type="button" data-uep-back-major>← 전공별 대학 찾기로</button></div>':uep08223TodayNav(university);
  const visibleAdmissions=admissions.filter(r=>String(r['UEP노출']||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N');
  const renderAdmissionCard=r=>'<article class="uep-uni-admission-card'+uep08261AdmissionCardClass(r)+'"><div class="uep-uni-badges">'+uep08223UniversityBadges(r)+'</div>'+uep08258AdmissionSupportBadge(r)+(String(r['모집인원']??'').trim()&&!uep08258AdmissionMeta(r).unavailable?'<span class="uep-admission-count-badge">모집 '+escapeHtml(String(r['모집인원']).trim())+'명</span>':'')+'<h4>'+escapeHtml(r['전형명']||'전형명 확인')+'</h4><p>'+escapeHtml(r['선발방식']||r['평가구조요약']||dashboardAdmissionMethod(r))+'</p>'+(String(r['추천/지원조건']??'').trim()?'<p class="uep-admission-support-condition"><b>지원조건</b> '+escapeHtml(String(r['추천/지원조건']).trim())+'</p>':'')+'</article>';
  const activeAdmissions=admissions.filter(uep08258AdmissionActive).sort(uep08258AdmissionCompare);
  const tabKey=r=>{const group=uep08261AdmissionGroup(r);return group==='교과'||group==='종합'?'early':group==='정시'?'regular':'other';};
  const renderTabPanel=(key,label)=>{
    const rows=activeAdmissions.filter(r=>tabKey(r)===key);
    const ordinary=rows.filter(r=>{const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(r);return !i.restricted&&!i.unavailable;});
    const regionalDoctor=rows.filter(r=>{const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(r);return uep08261RegionalDoctor(r)&&i.restricted&&!i.unavailable;});
    const detail=rows.filter(r=>{const i=uep08258AdmissionMeta(r);return i.unavailable||(i.restricted&&!regionalDoctor.includes(r));});
    const ordinaryHtml=ordinary.length?'<div class="uep-uni-admission-grid uep-uni-primary-grid">'+ordinary.map(renderAdmissionCard).join('')+'</div>':'<p class="uep-uni-admission-empty">'+escapeHtml(label)+' 일반 전형자료를 연결 중입니다.</p>';
    const regionalDoctorHtml=regionalDoctor.length?'<div class="uep-uni-featured-admissions"><div class="uep-uni-admission-subtitle">지역의사제</div><div class="uep-uni-admission-grid uep-uni-featured-grid">'+regionalDoctor.map(renderAdmissionCard).join('')+'</div></div>':'';
    const detailHtml=detail.length?'<details class="uep-uni-restricted-disclosure uep-uni-more-admissions"><summary>다른 전형 자세히 보기 <b>'+detail.length+'개</b><span>펼쳐보기</span></summary><div c
===== todayUniversity @ 805437 =====
rgetName));
    if(row)openDashboardUniversityDetail(row);
  };
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-region]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=event=>{
      event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
      const region=String(button.dataset.uepRegion||'').trim();
      if(!region)return;
      window.__uepAdmissionRegion=region;
      const first=dashboardAdmissionUniversities().find(r=>uep08223UniversityRegions(r).includes(region));
      if(first)openDashboardUniversityDetail(first);
    };
  });
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-university]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=event=>{event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();openByName(button.dataset.uepUniversity);};
  });
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-prev]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=event=>{event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();openByName(button.dataset.uepPrev);};
  });
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-next]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=event=>{event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();openByName(button.dataset.uepNext);};
  });
}
function dashboardStudentStatusCompactMarkup(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today),yesterday=new Date(today);yesterday.setDate(today.getDate()-1);const yesterdayKey=dateKey(yesterday);
  const official=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(todayKey));
  const late=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(row=>uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day)===todayKey));
  const night=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||'').slice(0,10)===yesterdayKey&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row)));
  const currentStudents=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(readonlyCache?.students||[]),u=dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity();
  const cards=[['status','late','공결/지각',`공결 ${new Set(official.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size} · 지각 ${new Set(late.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}`],['status','night','야자출결',`${new Set(night.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}/${currentStudents.length||259}`],['admission','basics','대입 이해','기초부터 실제 전형까지'],['admission','major','전공별 대학 찾기','전공·지역·전형·최저 검색'],['admission','university','오늘의 대학',u?.['대학명']||'대학 정보']];
  return `<div class="uep-student-status-compact uep-dashboard-five-cards">${cards.map(([type,key,label,value])=>`<button class="${type==='admission'?'dashboard-admission-card':''}" ${type==='status'?`data-dashboard-student-status="${key}"`:`data-dashboard-admission="${key}"`}><span>${label}</span><b>${escapeHtml(value)}</b></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function dashboardStudentStatusMarkup(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const yesterday=new Date(today);yesterday.setDate(today.getDate()-1);const yesterdayKey=dateKey(yesterday);
  const official=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(todayKey));
  const late=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(row=>uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day)===todayKey));
  const night=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||'').slice(0,10)===yesterdayKey&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row)));
  const reportRows=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(dashboardReportStatusRows());
  const targetGroups=dashboardReportGroups(reportRows);
  const submitted=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="complete");
  const progress=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="progress");
  const missing=targetGroups.filter(group=>dashboardReportGroupState(group).state==="missing");
  const scope=dashboardGradeScope();
  const currentStudents=filterRowsForDashboardStatus(readonlyCache?.students||[]);
  const officialCount=new Set(official.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const lateCount=new Set(late.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const nightCount=new Set(night.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size;
  const nightTotal=currentStudents.length||259;
  const metrics=[
    ['late','공결/지각',`${officialCount}/${lateCount}`,'attendance',`공결 ${officialCount}명 · 지각 ${lateCount}명`,'명'],
    ['night','야자출결',`${nightCount}/${nightTotal}`,'attendance','야자 출결 학생','명'],
    ['submitted','제출완료',submitted.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 제출완료 학생','명'],
    ['progress','제출중',progress.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 일부 제출 학생','명'],
    ['report','미제출',missing.length,'programs','선택활동 보고서 미제출 학생','명']
  ];
  return `<div class="uep-student-scope"><span>조회 기준 · <b>${escapeHtml(scope.label)}</b></span><strong>선택활동 대상 <b>${targetGroups.length}</b>명</strong><em>완료 ${submitted.length}명 · 제출중 ${progress.length}명 · 미제출 ${missing.length}명</em></div><div class="uep-student-kpis">${metrics.map(([kind,label,count,page,desc,unit])=>`<button class="kpi-${kind}" data-dashboard-student-status="${kind}" data-page="${page}" title="${desc} 조회"><span>${label}</span><b>${count}</b><small>${kind==='late'?desc:unit}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* UEP_08222_DIALOG_WRAPPER */
function uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog(){
 const flow=document.querySelector('.admission-learning-flow,.uep-admission-university-detail');if(!flow)return;
 let node=flow.parentElement;for(let i=0;node&&i<6;i++,node=node.parentElement){const w=node.getBoundingClientRect().width;if(w>650&&w<window.innerWidth*.96){node.style.width='min(1460px, calc(100vw - 28px))';node.style.maxWidth='min(1460px, calc(100vw - 28px))';break;}}
}
function openDashboardAdmissionDialog(title,html){
  const isUniversity=String(html||'').includes('주요 전형과 선발방식');
  if(isUniversity){
    const origin=window.__uepAdmissionReturn||'today';
    const label=origin==='types'?'전형 이해로':'오늘의 대학으로';
    const back=`<div class="uep-admission-backbar"><button type="button" class="uep-admission-back" data-uep-admission-back="${origin}">← ${label}</button></div>`;
    html=back+`<div class="uep-admission-university-detail">${html}</div>`;
  }
  const result=openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase(title,html);
  setTimeout(()=>{uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog();document.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-admission-back]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const o=btn.dataset.uepAdmissionBack;window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';if(o==='types')openDashboardAdmissionTypes();else if(typeof openDashboardTodayUniversity==='function')openDashboardTodayUniversity();else if(typeof openDashboardUniversity==='function')openDashboardUniversity();});},20);
  return result;
}

/* UEP_08222_VERSION_UPDATE_INDICATOR */
(function(){
 const CURRENT='0.82.77';
 const parse=v=>String(v||'').replace(/^v/i,'').split('.').map(n=>parseInt(n,10)||0);
 const newer=(a,b)=>{const A=parse(a),B=parse(b);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){if((A[i]||0)!==(B[i]||0))return (A[i]||0)>(B[i]||0)}return false};
 function versionPill(){return [...document.querySelectorAll('span,button,div')].find(el=>/^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test((el.textContent||'').trim())&&el.children.length===0)}
 function removeDuplicate(){document.getElementById('uep-global-version-badge')?.remove();document.querySelectorAll('.uep-top-version-badge').forEach(el=>el.remove());[...document.querySelectorAll('span,button,div')].forEach(el=>{if(/^UEP v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test((el.textContent||'').trim())&&el.children.length===0)el.remove();});}
 async function check(){removeDuplicate();const pill=versionPill();if(!pill)return;try{const r=await fetch('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-policy.json?ts='+Date.now(),{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return;const p=await r.json();const latest=String(p.latestVersion||'').replace(/^v/i,'');if(newer(latest,CURRENT)){pill.textContent='v'+CURRENT+' → v'+latest;pill.classList.add('uep-version-update-available');pill.title='새 업데이트 '+latest+' 사용 가능';}else{pill.textContent='v'+CURRENT;pill.classList.remove('uep-version-update-available');}}catch(e){pill.textContent='v'+CURRENT;}}
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(check,1200),{once:true});else setTimeout(check,1200);setInterval(check,30*60*1000);
})();

/* UEP_08222_DIALOG_WRAPPER */
function uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog(){
 const flow=document.querySelector('.admission-learning-flow,.uep-admission-university-detail');if(!flow)return;
 let node=flow.parentElement;for(let i=0;node&&i<6;i++,node=node.parentElement){const w=node.getBoundingClientRect().width;if(w>650&&w<window.innerWidth*.96){node.style.width='min(1460px, calc(100vw - 28px))';node.style.maxWidth='min(1460px, calc(100vw - 28px))';break;}}
}
function openDashboardAdmissionDialog(title,html){
  const isUniversity=String(html||'').includes('주요 전형과 선발방식');
  if(isUniversity){
    const origin=window.__uepAdmissionReturn||'today';
    const label=origin==='types'?'전형 이해로':'오늘의 대학으로';
    const back=`<div class="uep-admission-backbar"><button type="button" class="uep-admission-back" data-uep-admission-back="${origin}">← ${label}</button></div>`;
    html=back+`<div class="uep-admission-university-detail">${html}</div>`;
  }
  const result=openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase(title,html);
  setTimeout(()=>{uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog();document.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-admission-back]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const o=btn.dataset.uepAdmissionBack;window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';if(o==='types')openDashboardAdmissionTypes();else if(typeof openDashboardTodayUniversity==='function')openDashboardTodayUniversity();else if(typeof openDashboardUniversity==='function')openDashboardUniversity();});},20);
  return result;
}

(function(){
  const rows=(key,sheet)=>{try{return dashboardAdmissionRows(key,sheet)||[]}catch(e){return (readonlyCache&&readonlyCache[key])||[]}};
  const norm=v=>{try{return dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(v||'')}catch(e){return String(v||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/대학교/g,'대')}};
  const campuses=v=>String(v||'').split(/[\/·,]/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const activeUniversities=()=>rows('universityAdmissions','56_대학입시마스터').filter(r=>String(r['사용여부']||'Y').toUpperCase()!=='N').sort((a,b)=>(Number(a['노출순서'])||9999)-(Number(b['노출순서'])||9999)||(String(a['대학ID']||'').localeCompare(String(b['대학ID']||''))));
  window.uepAdmissionsGraph={universities:activeUniversities,types:()=>rows('admissionTypes','53_전형이해'),subtypes:()=>rows('admissionSubtypes','53A_전형세부유형DB'),typeUniversities:()=>rows('admissionTypeUniversities','53B_전형유형별대학DB'),minimums:()=>rows('admissionMinimumRows','54_수능최저DB'),gradeCalcs:()=>rows('admissionGradeCalcs','57_내신산정DB')};
  const originalToday=typeof dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity==='function'?dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity:null;
  if(originalToday){dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity=function(){const list=activeUniversities();if(!list.length)return originalToday();const day=Math.floor(Date.now()/86400000);return list[((day%list.length)+list.length)%list.length];};}
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{try{const list=activeUniversities();console.info('[UEP admissions] 56→53B/54/57 linked universities',list.length);}catch(e){}},{once:true});
})();

function admissionManagementRows(group,filter=admissionManagementFilter){
  const order={core:0,growth:1,interest:2,general:3};
  return admissionManagementDataset(group)
    .filter(x=>x.stage!=='general'&&(filter==='all'||x.stage===filter))
    .sort((a,b)=>order[a.stage]-order[b.stage]||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));
}
function admissionManagementMarkup(group){
  const fallbackRule=uepManagementRule(group);
  const dataset=admissionManagementDataset(group),standard=dataset[0]?.standard||{name:fallbackRule.label,internal:fallbackRule.internal,mockText:fallbackRule.mockText};
  const managed=dataset.filter(x=>x.stage!=='general');
  const counts={all:managed.length,core:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='core').length,growth:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='growth').length,interest:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='interest').length};
  const rows=admissionManagementRows(group,admissionManagementFilter);
  const stageLabel={all:'전체 관리 대상',core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'}[admissionManagementFilter]||'전체 관리 대상';
  const filters=`<div class="admission-manage-filters growth-filters"><button data-admission-manage-filter="all" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='all'?'active':''}">전체 <b>${counts.all}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="core" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='core'?'active':''}">핵심관리 <b>${counts.core}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="growth" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='growth'?'active':''}">성장관리 <b>${counts.growth}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="interest" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='interest'?'active':''}">관심관리 <b>${counts.interest}</b></button></div>`;
  const stageName={core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'};
  return `<div class="admission-manage-note growth-note"><b>${standard.name} 성장관리 기준</b> · 내신과 모의고사 기준을 함께 충족하면 핵심관리, 양쪽에 근거가 있으나 모의 일부기준만 충족하면 성장관리, 내신 또는 모의 한 종류만 충족하면 관심관리로 분류합니다. 내신은 실제 9등급 평균 ${standard.internal} 이내와 동일한 5등급 평균 그룹을 보호대상으로 포함합니다.</div>${filters}<div function admissionManagementRows(group,filter=admissionManagementFilter){
  const order={core:0,growth:1,interest:2,general:3};
  return admissionManagementDataset(group)
    .filter(x=>x.stage!=='general'&&(filter==='all'||x.stage===filter))
    .sort((a,b)=>order[a.stage]-order[b.stage]||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));
}
function admissionManagementMarkup(group){

  const dataset=admissionManagementDataset(group),standard=dataset[0]?.standard||{name:fallbackRule.label,internal:fallbackRule.internal,mockText:fallbackRule.mockText};
  const managed=dataset.filter(x=>x.stage!=='general');
  const counts={all:managed.length,core:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='core').length,growth:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='growth').length,interest:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='interest').length};
  const rows=admissionManagementRows(group,admissionManagementFilter);

  const stageName={core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'};

function admissionsView() {
  const classes=availableClasses();
  const growthMode=admissionMode==='growth';
  const managementMode=["inseoul","regional"].includes(admissionMode);
  const mockMode=admissionMode==="mock";
  const label={growth:"성장관리센터",conversion:"등급환산",minimum:"수능최저",cuts:"대학별 입결",combined:"종합분석",mock:"모의고사 입시분석",inseoul:"인서울 관리",regional:"지거국 관리"}[admissionMode]||'성장관리센터';
  const needsUniversity=["minimum","cuts","combined"].includes(admissionMode);
  const allowUniversityScope=!growthMode&&!managementMode&&admissionMode!=="conversion";
  const universities=admissionUniversities();
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs admission-growth-tabs"><button data-admission-mode="growth" class="${growthMode?"active":""}">01 성장관리센터</button><button data-admission-mode="inseoul" class="${admissionMode==="inseoul"?"active":""}">02 인서울관리</button><button data-admission-mode="regional" class="${admissionMode==="regional"?"active":""}">03 지거국관리</button><button data-admission-mode="combined" class="${admissionMode==="combined"?"active":""}">04 종합분석</button><button data-admission-mode="mock" class="${mockMode?"active":""}">05 모의고사</button><button data-admission-mode="minimum" class="${admissionMode==="minimum"?"active":""}">06 수능최저</button><button data-admission-mode="cuts" class="${admissionMode==="cuts"?"active":""}">대학별 입결</button><button data-admission-mode="conversion" class="${admissionMode==="conversion"?"active":""}">등급환산</button></div>`;
  if(mockMode){
    return `<div class="module-page admission-mock-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>05</span><div><h3>모의고사 입시분석</h3><p>학생의 누적 모의고사를 내신·수능최저·대학입결과 연결해 입시 상담 기준으로 분석합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label>학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">입시 모의분석 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="admissionQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>◎</span><b>입시 분석할 학생을 선택하세요.</b><small>누적 모의고사와 내신·수능최저·대학입결을 함께 연결합니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
  }
  if(growthMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>01</span><div><h3>성장관리센터</h3><p>오늘 새롭게 발굴된 학생, 입시 상담이력, 다음 확인일을 한곳에서 관리합니다. 상담 원본은 학생정보 대시보드에 저장되고 이곳에서는 입시 유형만 활용합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${growthCenterMarkup()}</div>`;
  if(managementMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='inseoul'?'02':'03'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>내신과 모의고사 기준 충족 정도에 따라 핵심·성장·관심 관리군을 구성하고 이력을 지속 관리합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${admissionManagementMarkup(admissionMode==="inseoul"?"inseoul":"regional")}</div>`;

  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='combined'?'04':admissionMode==='minimum'?'06':'·'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>학생별 내신·모의고사·수능최저·대학입결을 연결해 상담 근거를 확인합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>조회방식<select class="filter" id="admissionScope"><option value="individual">개인 학생 조회</option>${allowUniversityScope?'<option value="university">대학 기준 학생 찾기</option>':''}</select></label><label id="admissionClassLabel">반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="admissionStudentLabel">학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label>${needsUniversity?`<label id="admissionUniversityLabel">대학 선택<input class="search compact" id="admissionUniversity" list="admissionUniversityOptions" placeholder="대학명 입력 또는 선택"><datalist id="admissionUniversityOptions">${universities.map(name=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}"></option>`).join("")}</datalist><small>연결된 수능최저·입결 DB 기준 자동완성</small></label>`:""}<button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">${label} 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록
===== data-admission @ 509810 =====
><b>①</b> 9등급 2.5 경계</span><span><b>②</b> 9등급 4.0 경계</span><span><b>③</b> 내신↔모의 불균형</span><span><b>④</b> 프로그램 참여 부족</span></div></section><section class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>입시 상담이력</strong></div><small>작성자 본인 상담 기록</small></div>${admissionRecords.length?admissionRecords.slice(0,8).map(row=>`<article class="growth-counsel-row"><div><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</b><small>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').slice(0,10))}</small></div><p>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</p><span>${escapeHtml(row.status||'진행중')}</span></article>`).join(''):'<div class="query-empty"><b>입시 상담이력이 없습니다.</b><span>상담하기에서 기록하면 작성자 본인에게만 표시됩니다.</span></div>'}</section></div></div>`;
}

  const classes=availableClasses();

===== admissionUniversity @ 487322 =====
}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.className)}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(exams.join(" · "))}</span><strong>${item.minMatches.length}</strong><span><b>${escapeHtml(first.rule.name||first.rule.type||"")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(first.check.detail)}</small></span></div>`;}).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>현재 수능최저 충족 학생이 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div>`;
  const combinedTable=`<div class="data-card cohort-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${escapeHtml(university)} 내신·수능최저 동시 충족</strong></div><small>현재 자료에서 두 조건을 모두 충족한 학생</small></div><div class="data-head cohort-combined-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>실제 9등급</span><span>내신 충족</span><span>최저 충족 모의고사</span></div>${combinedRows.map(item=>`<div class="data-row cohort-combined-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.className)}반</span><strong>${item.base.actual9?.toFixed(2)||"-"}</strong><span>${item.cutMatches.length}개 모집단위</span><span>${escapeHtml([...new Set(item.minMatches.map(x=>x.exam))].join(" · "))}</span></div>`).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>동시 충족 학생이 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div>`;
  const summary=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${escapeHtml(university)}</b>대학 기준</span><span><b>${internalRows.length}</b>내신 충족</span><span><b>${minimumRows.length}</b>최저 충족</span><span><b>${combinedRows.length}</b>동시 충족</span></div>`;
  if(mode==="minimum") return summary+minimumTable;
  if(mode==="cuts") return summary+internalTable;
  return summary+combinedTable+internalTable+minimumTable;
}

function runAdmissionQuery() {
  const scope=$("#admissionScope")?.value || "individual";
  const studentId=$("#admissionStudent")?.value || "";
  const universityInput=($("#admissionUniversity")?.value || "").trim();
  const target=$("#admissionQueryResult");
  const students=readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const rules=(readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums || []).filter(x=>universityMatches(x.university,universityInput));
  const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts || []).filter(x=>universityMatches(x.university,universityInput));
  if(scope==="university") {
    if(!universityInput){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>대학을 선택하세요.</b><span>대학명을 입력하거나 자동완성 목록에서 선택하면 충족 학생을 모아 보여줍니다.</span></div>`;return;}
    if(!rules.length&&!cuts.length){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>연결 자료에서 대학을 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>자동완성 목록의 대학명을 선택하세요.</span></div>`;return;}
    target.innerHTML=universityCohortMarkup(universityInput,admissionMode,rules,cuts);
    return;
  }
  if(!studentId){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반 필터는 선택 사항이며, 학생을 고른 뒤 조회하세요.</span></div>`;return;}
  const student=students.find(x=>x.id===studentId);
  if(!student){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`;return;}
  const base=admissionBase(student.id);
  const head=`<div class="student-admission-head"><div><small>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} · ${escapeHtml(student.className)}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(student.name)} 입시 조회</h3></div><span class="state">${escapeHtml(base.preferred || "내신 자료")} · 5등급제/실제9등급</span></div>`;
  const actualDetailTable=`<div class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>석차 기반 실제 9등급</strong></div><small>석차 ÷ 수강자수 누적비율 기준</small></div><div class="data-head actual-nine-grid"><span>과목</span><span>원점수</span><span>5등급</span><span>석차/수강자</span><span>상위비율</span><span>실제 9등급</span></div>${base.actualDetails.map(({row,result})=>`<div class="data-row actual-nine-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</b><span>${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(row.level,2)}</span><span>${result.rank}/${result.enrollment}</span><span>${result.percentile.toFixed(1)}%</span><span class="state">${result.grade}등급</span></div>`).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>석차·수강자수 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>산출제외 과목은 실제 9등급 계산에서 제외합니다.</span></div>'}</div>`;
  const conversion=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${base.avg5==null?"-":base.avg5.toFixed(2)}</b>5등급제 평균</span><span><b>${base.actual9==null?"-":base.actual9.toFixed(2)}</b>실제 9등급 평균</span><span><b>${base.actualDetails.length}</b>산출과목</span><span><b>${escapeHtml(base.preferred||"-")}</b>기준고사</span></div>${actualDetailTable}`;

function studentMatches(item, student) {
  const studentId=String(student?.id||"").trim();
  const itemStudentId=String(item?.studentId||item?.학생ID||"").trim();
  if(studentId && itemStudentId && itemStudentId===studentId)return true;
  
  const no=String(student?.studentNo||"").replace(/\\.0$/,"" ).trim();
  const itemNo=String(item?.studentNo||item?.학번||item?.number||"").replace(/\\.0$/,"" ).trim(); return Boolean(no)&&Boolean(itemNo)&&itemNo===no;
}
function studentEntryDate(student){
  const raw=student?.entryDate||student?.statusDate||student?.moveInDate||student?.admissionDate||student?.enrollmentDate||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentLinkedRecordDate(item){
  const raw=item?.date||item?.day||item?.activityDate||item?.attendanceDate||item?.submittedAt||item?.finalSubmittedAt||item?.startDate||item?.endDate||item?.recordDate||item?.createdAt||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student){
  const entry=studentEntryDate(student);
  if(!entry||!/전입/.test(String(student?.status||student?.enrollmentStatus||student?.enrollmentReason||"")))return true;
  const recordDate=studentLinkedRecordDate(item);
  return !recordDate||recordDate>=entry;
}
function normalizedVolunteerTitle(value){
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/^\s*\((?:자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?|진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동|동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동)\)\s*/,"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(/중식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "중식도우미";
  if(/석식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "석식도우미";
  return text.replace(/급식/g,"");
}
function volunteerIdentity(item){
  return [String(item?.date||item?.endDate||item?.startDate||""),normalizedVolunteerTitle(item?.title||item?.type||"")].join("|");
}
function isVolunteerActivity(item){
  return /봉사|도우미|필사집|교과도우미|수업\s*지원/.test(`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`);
}
function isTrueClubActivity(item){
  const text=`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`;
  return /동아리/.test(text)&&!isVolunteerActivity(item)&&!/\(동아리활동\)/.test(text);
}

  if(/석식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "석식도우미";
  return text.replace(/급식/g,"");
}
function volunteerIdentity(item){
  return [String(item?.date||item?.endDate||item?.startDate||""),normalizedVolunteerTitle(item?.title||item?.type||"")].join("|");
}
function isVolunteerActivity(item){
  return /봉사|도우미|필사집|교과도우미|수업\s*지원/.test(`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`);
}
function isTrueClubActivity(item){
  const text=`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`;
  return /동아리/.test(text)&&!isVolunteerActivity(item)&&!/\(동아리활동\)/.test(text);
}

function studentMatches(item, student) {
  const studentId=String(student?.id||"").trim();
  const itemStudentId=String(item?.studentId||item?.학생ID||"").trim();
  if(studentId && itemStudentId && itemStudentId===studentId)return true;
  
  const no=String(student?.studentNo||"").replace(/\\.0$/,"" ).trim();
  const itemNo=String(item?.studentNo||item?.학번||item?.number||"").replace(/\\.0$/,"" ).trim(); return Boolean(no)&&Boolean(itemNo)&&itemNo===no;
}
function studentEntryDate(student){
  const raw=student?.entryDate||student?.statusDate||student?.moveInDate||student?.admissionDate||student?.enrollmentDate||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentLinkedRecordDate(item){
  const raw=item?.date||item?.day||item?.activityDate||item?.attendanceDate||item?.submittedAt||item?.finalSubmittedAt||item?.startDate||item?.endDate||item?.recordDate||item?.createdAt||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student){
  const entry=studentEntryDate(student);
  if(!entry||!/전입/.test(String(student?.status||student?.enrollmentStatus||student?.enrollmentReason||"")))return true;
  const recordDate=studentLinkedRecordDate(item);
  return !recordDate||recordDate>=entry;
}
function normalizedVolunteerTitle(value){
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/^\s*\((?:자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?|진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동|동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동)\)\s*/,"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(/중식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "중식도우미";
  if(/석식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "석식도우미";
  return text.replace(/급식/g,"");
}
function volunteerIdentity(item){
  return [String(item?.date||item?.endDate||item?.startDate||""),normalizedVolunteerTitle(item?.title||item?.type||"")].join("|");
}

function dashboardAdmissionMethod(row){
  const text=[row['선발방식'],row['평가구조요약'],row['핵심평가'],row['주요평가자료'],row['한줄요약'],row['상담표시문구']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  if(row['선발방식'])return String(row['선발방식']);if(row['평가구조요약'])return String(row['평가구조요약']);
  const out=[];if(/교과|내신/.test(text))out.push('내신');if(/수능최저|최저/.test(text))out.push('수능최저');if(/서류|학생부/.test(text))out.push('서류');if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');if(/논술/.test(text))out.unshift('논술');if(/수능/.test(text)&&/정시|수능/.test(String(row['전형유형']||'')))return '수능100 또는 영역별 반영';return [...new Set(out)].join(' + ')||'대학별 방식 확인';
}

function uep08253AdmissionCounselPointsFor(university){
  const norm=dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(university?.['대학명']||'');
  return dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionCounselPoints','56A_대학상담포인트DB').filter(r=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm&&String(r['사용여부']||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N').sort((a,b)=>String(a['작성일']||'').localeCompare(String(b['작성일']||'')));
}
function uep08253CanEditAdmissionCounsel(){return ['admin','grade_head','grade_manager'].includes(currentRoleId());}
function uep08253AdmissionCounselMarkup(university){
  const base=String(university?.['담임상담체크']||university?.['카드한줄']||'학생의 내신·선택과목·모의고사·수업 탐구를 대학 전형 구조와 함께 확인합니다.').trim();
  const points=uep08253AdmissionCounselPointsFor(university);
  const extra=points.map(r=>{const scope=[r['전형명'],r['모집단위']].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).filter(Boolean).join(' · ');return '<div class="uep-uni-counsel-point custom"><small>학년부 추가'+(scope?' · '+escapeHtml(scope):'')+'</small><p>'+escapeHtml(r['상담포인트']||'')+'</p></div>';}).join('');
  return '<section class="uep-uni-counsel"><div class="uep-uni-counsel-head"><b>담임 상담 포인트</b>'+(uep08253CanEditAdmissionCounsel()?'<button type="button" class="uep-uni-counsel-edit-button" data-uep-counsel-edit>수정·추가</button>':'')+'</div><div class="uep-uni-counsel-points"><div class="uep-uni-counsel-point base"><small>UEP 기본</small><p>'+escapeHtml(base)+'</p></div>'+extra+'</div></section>';
}

function uep08253AdmissionCounselPointsFor(university){
  const norm=dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(university?.['대학명']||'');
  return dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionCounselPoints','56A_대학상담포인트DB').filter(r=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm&&String(r['사용여부']||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N').sort((a,b)=>String(a['작성일']||'').localeCompare(String(b['작성일']||'')));
}
function uep08253CanEditAdmissionCounsel(){return ['admin','grade_head','grade_manager'].includes(currentRoleId());}
function uep08253AdmissionCounselMarkup(university){
  const base=String(university?.['담임상담체크']||university?.['카드한줄']||'학생의 내신·선택과목·모의고사·수업 탐구를 대학 전형 구조와 함께 확인합니다.').trim();
  const points=uep08253AdmissionCounselPointsFor(university);
  const extra=points.map(r=>{const scope=[r['전형명'],r['모집단위']].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).filter(Boolean).join(' · ');return '<div class="uep-uni-counsel-point custom"><small>학년부 추가'+(scope?' · '+escapeHtml(scope):'')+'</small><p>'+escapeHtml(r['상담포인트']||'')+'</p></div>';}).join('');
  return '<section class="uep-uni-counsel"><div class="uep-uni-counsel-head"><b>담임 상담 포인트</b>'+(uep08253CanEditAdmissionCounsel()?'<button type="button" class="uep-uni-counsel-edit-button" data-uep-counsel-edit>수정·추가</button>':'')+'</div><div class="uep-uni-counsel-points"><div class="uep-uni-counsel-point base"><small>UEP 기본</small><p>'+escapeHtml(base)+'</p></div>'+extra+'</div></section>';
}

function uep08221ShowReleaseNotes(){
  if(String(APP_VERSION)!=='0.82.21') return;
  const key='uep:release-notes:'+APP_VERSION;
  try{if(localStorage.getItem(key)==='shown')return;}catch{}
  if(document.getElementById('uep-release-notes-overlay'))return;
  const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.id='uep-release-notes-overlay';
  overlay.innerHTML=`<div class="uep-release-notes-card"><div class="uep-release-notes-head"><div><small>UPDATE</small><h2>UEP v${escapeHtml(APP_VERSION)} 수정사항</h2><p>이번 버전에 반영된 내용을 확인하세요.</p></div><button type="button" aria-label="닫기" class="uep-release-notes-close">×</button></div><div class="uep-release-notes-list">${UEP_08221_RELEASE_NOTES.map((item,i)=>`<div><span>${i+1}</span><p>${escapeHtml(item)}</p></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="uep-release-notes-actions"><button type="button" class="primary">확인</button></div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  const close=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(key,'shown');}catch{}uep08221CloseReleaseNotes();};
  overlay.querySelector('.uep-release-notes-close')?.addEventListener('click',close);
  overlay.querySelector('.uep-release-notes-actions .primary')?.addEventListener('click',close);
}
function uep08221RemoveAdmissionBack(){document.getElementById('uep-admission-back-button')?.remove();}
function uep08221ShowAdmissionBack(){
  uep08221RemoveAdmissionBack();
  const button=document.createElement('button');button.id='uep-admission-back-button';button.type='button';button.innerHTML='← 전형 이해로 돌아가기';
  button.onclick=()=>{uep08221RemoveAdmissionBack();openDashboardAdmissionTypes();};
  document.body.appendChild(button);
}

function uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog(){

function openDashboardAdmissionDialog(title,html){
  const isUniversity=String(html||'').includes('주요 전형과 선발방식');
  if(isUniversity){
    const origin=window.__uepAdmissionReturn||'today';
    const label=origin==='types'?'전형 이해로':'오늘의 대학으로';
    const back=`<div class="uep-admission-backbar"><button type="button" class="uep-admission-back" data-uep-admission-back="${origin}">← ${label}</button></div>`;
    html=back+`<div class="uep-admission-university-detail">${html}</div>`;
  }
  const result=openDashboardAdmissionDialogBase(title,html);
  setTimeout(()=>{uep08222ExpandAdmissionDialog();document.querySelectorAll('[data-uep-admission-back]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const o=btn.dataset.uepAdmissionBack;window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';if(o==='types')openDashboardAdmissionTypes();else if(typeof openDashboardTodayUniversity==='function')openDashboardTodayUniversity();else if(typeof openDashboardUniversity==='function')openDashboardUniversity();});},20);
  return result;
}

  const night=filterRowsForDashboardStatus((readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||'').slice(0,10)===yesterdayKey&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row)));

}

function openAdmissionStageBadge(studentId,group,stage){
  const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id||'')===String(studentId||''));if(!student)return;
  const row=admissionManagementDataset(group).find(x=>String(x.student?.id||'')===String(studentId||''));if(!row)return;
  const label=`${group==='inseoul'?'인서울':'지거국'} · ${stage==='core'?'핵심관리':stage==='growth'?'성장관리':'관심관리'}`;
  $('#drawerTitle').textContent=`${student.name} · ${label}`;
  const fallbackRule=uepManagementRule(group);
  const standard=row.standard||{internal:fallbackRule.internal,mockText:fallbackRule.mockText};
  const gaps=[];
  if(!row.internalOk&&row.base.actual9!=null)gaps.push(`내신 ${row.base.actual9.toFixed(2)} → ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 필요`);
  if(!row.mockCore){
    const main=(row.mock.rows||[]).filter(r=>/국어|수학|영어/.test(String(r.subject||''))).map(r=>({subject:r.subject,level:Number(r.level)})).filter(r=>Number.isFinite(r.level));
    if(group==='inseoul'){
      main.filter(r=>r.level>3).forEach(r=>gaps.push(`${r.subject} ${r.level}등급 → 3등급 이내 필요`));
      const within2=main.filter(r=>r.level<=2).length; if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 중 2등급 이내 영역 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
    }else{
      main.filter(r=>r.level>5).forEach(r=>gaps.push(`${r.subject} ${r.level}등급 → 5등급 이내 필요`));
      const within4=main.filter(r=>r.level<=4).length; if(within4<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 중 4등급 이내 영역 ${within4}개 → 2개 필요`);
    }
  }
  if(!gaps.length&&stage!=='core')gaps.push('핵심관리 기준과의 세부 차이를 최신 성적에서 다시 확인');
  $('#drawerBody').innerHTML=`<div class="data-card badge-evidence-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${label}</strong></div><small>성적 기반 관리단계</small></div><div class="badge-evidence-list"><span><b>내신</b><em>${row.base.actual9==null?'-':row.base.actual9.toFixed(2)} · ${row.internalOk?'기준 충족':'기준 미충족'}</em></span><span><b>최근 모의고사</b><em>${escapeHtml(row.mock.detail||'자료 없음')}</em></span></div><p><b>선정 근거</b> · ${row.reasons.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ')}</p>${stage!=='core'?`<div class="badge-next-step"><b>핵심관리까지 부족한 부분</b>${gaps.map(x=>`<span>${escapeHtml(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`:''}<button type="button" class="btn primary" data-badge-route="admissions">해당 페이지로 이동</button></div>`;
  $('#drawer')?.classList.remove('hidden');$('#drawerBackdrop')?.classList.remove('hidden');
  $('#drawerBody [data-badge-route]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{selectedStudentId=student.id||student.studentNo||'';render('admissions');$('#drawer')?.classList.add('hidden');$('#drawerBackdrop')?.classList.add('hidden');window.scrollTo(0,0);});
}

function admissionsView() {
  const classes=availableClasses();
  const growthMode=admissionMode==='growth';
  const managementMode=["inseoul","regional"].includes(admissionMode);
  const mockMode=admissionMode==="mock";
  const label={growth:"성장관리센터",conversion:"등급환산",minimum:"수능최저",cuts:"대학별 입결",combined:"종합분석",mock:"모의고사 입시분석",inseoul:"인서울 관리",regional:"지거국 관리"}[admissionMode]||'성장관리센터';
  const needsUniversity=["minimum","cuts","combined"].includes(admissionMode);
  const allowUniversityScope=!growthMode&&!managementMode&&admissionMode!=="conversion";
  const universities=admissionUniversities();
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs admission-growth-tabs"><button data-admission-mode="growth" class="${growthMode?"active":""}">01 성장관리센터</button><button data-admission-mode="inseoul" class="${admissionMode==="inseoul"?"active":""}">02 인서울관리</button><button data-admission-mode="regional" class="${admissionMode==="regional"?"active":""}">03 지거국관리</button><button data-admission-mode="combined" class="${admissionMode==="combined"?"active":""}">04 종합분석</button><button data-admission-mode="mock" class="${mockMode?"active":""}">05 모의고사</button><button data-admission-mode="minimum" class="${admissionMode==="minimum"?"active":""}">06 수능최저</button><button data-admission-mode="cuts" class="${admissionMode==="cuts"?"active":""}">대학별 입결</button><button data-admission-mode="conversion" class="${admissionMode==="conversion"?"active":""}">등급환산</button></div>`;
  if(mockMode){

    return `<div class="module-page admission-mock-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>05</span><div><h3>모의고사 입시분석</h3><p>학생의 누적 모의고사를 내신·수능최저·대학입결과 연결해 입시 상담 기준으로 분석합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label>학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">입시 모의분석 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="admissionQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>◎</span><b>입시 분석할 학생을 선택하세요.</b><small>누적 모의고사와 내신·수능최저·대학입결을 함께 연결합니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
  }
  if(growthMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>01</span><div><h3>성장관리센터</h3><p>오늘 새롭게 발굴된 학생, 입시 상담이력, 다음 확인일을 한곳에서 관리합니다. 상담 원본은 학생정보 대시보드에 저장되고 이곳에서는 입시 유형만 활용합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${growthCenterMarkup()}</div>`;
  if(managementMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='inseoul'?'02':'03'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>내신과 모의고사 기준 충족 정도에 따라 핵심·성장·관심 관리군을 구성하고 이력을 지속 관리합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${admissionManagementMarkup(admissionMode==="inseoul"?"inseoul":"regional")}</div>`;
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='combined'?'04':admissionMode==='minimum'?'06':'·'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>학생별 내신·모의고사·수능최저·대학입결을 연결해 상담 근거를 확인합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>조회방식<select class="filter" id="admissionScope"><option value="individual">개인 학생 조회</option>${allowUniversityScope?'<option value="university">대학 기준 학생 찾기</option>':''}</select></label><label id="admissionClassLabel">반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="admissionStudentLabel">학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label>${needsUniversity?`<label id="admissionUniversityLabel">대학 선택<input class="search compact" id="admissionUniversity" list="admissionUniversityOptions" placeholder="대학명 입력 또는 선택"><datalist id="admissionUniversityOptions">${universities.map(name=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}"></option>`).join("")}</datalist><small>연결된 수능최저·입결 DB 기준 자동완성</small></label>`:""}<button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">${label} 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록
===== data-admission-mode @ 509810 =====
><b>①</b> 9등급 2.5 경계</span><span><b>②</b> 9등급 4.0 경계</span><span><b>③</b> 내신↔모의 불균형</span><span><b>④</b> 프로그램 참여 부족</span></div></section><section class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>입시 상담이력</strong></div><small>작성자 본인 상담 기록</small></div>${admissionRecords.length?admissionRecords.slice(0,8).map(row=>`<article class="growth-counsel-row"><div><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</b><small>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').slice(0,10))}</small></div><p>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</p><span>${escapeHtml(row.status||'진행중')}</span></article>`).join(''):'<div class="query-empty"><b>입시 상담이력이 없습니다.</b><span>상담하기에서 기록하면 작성자 본인에게만 표시됩니다.</span></div>'}</section></div></div>`;
}

  const needsUniversity=["minimum","cuts","combined"].includes(admissionMode);
  const allowUniversityScope=!growthMode&&!managementMode&&admissionMode!=="conversion";
  const universities=admissionUniversities();
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs admission-growth-tabs"><button data-admission-mode="growth" class="${growthMode?"active":""}">01 성장관리센터</button><button data-admission-mode="inseoul" class="${admissionMode==="inseoul"?"active":""}">02 인서울관리</button><button data-admission-mode="regional" class="${admissionMode==="regional"?"active":""}">03 지거국관리</button><button data-admission-mode="combined" class="${admissionMode==="combined"?"active":""}">04 종합분석</button><button data-admission-mode="mock" class="${mockMode?"active":""}">05 모의고사</button><button data-admission-mode="minimum" class="${admissionMode==="minimum"?"active":""}">06 수능최저</button><button data-admission-mode="cuts" class="${admissionMode==="cuts"?"active":""}">대학별 입결</button><button data-admission-mode="conversion" class="${admissionMode==="conversion"?"active":""}">등급환산</button></div>`;

function admissionsView() {
  const classes=availableClasses();
  const growthMode=admissionMode==='growth';
  const managementMode=["inseoul","regional"].includes(admissionMode);
  const mockMode=admissionMode==="mock";
  const label={growth:"성장관리센터",conversion:"등급환산",minimum:"수능최저",cuts:"대학별 입결",combined:"종합분석",mock:"모의고사 입시분석",inseoul:"인서울 관리",regional:"지거국 관리"}[admissionMode]||'성장관리센터';
  const needsUniversity=["minimum","cuts","combined"].includes(admissionMode);
  const allowUniversityScope=!growthMode&&!managementMode&&admissionMode!=="conversion";
  const universities=admissionUniversities();
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs admission-growth-tabs"><button data-admission-mode="growth" class="${growthMode?"active":""}">01 성장관리센터</button><button data-admission-mode="inseoul" class="${admissionMode==="inseoul"?"active":""}">02 인서울관리</button><button data-admission-mode="regional" class="${admissionMode==="regional"?"active":""}">03 지거국관리</button><button data-admission-mode="combined" class="${admissionMode==="combined"?"active":""}">04 종합분석</button><button data-admission-mode="mock" class="${mockMode?"active":""}">05 모의고사</button><button data-admission-mode="minimum" class="${admissionMode==="minimum"?"active":""}">06 수능최저</button><button data-admission-mode="cuts" class="${admissionMode==="cuts"?"active":""}">대학별 입결</button><button data-admission-mode="conversion" class="${admissionMode==="conversion"?"active":""}">등급환산</button></div>`;
  if(mockMode){
    return `<div class="module-page admission-mock-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>05</span><div><h3>모의고사 입시분석</h3><p>학생의 누적 모의고사를 내신·수능최저·대학입결과 연결해 입시 상담 기준으로 분석합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label>학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">입시 모의분석 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="admissionQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>◎</span><b>입시 분석할 학생을 선택하세요.</b><small>누적 모의고사와 내신·수능최저·대학입결을 함께 연결합니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
  }
  if(growthMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>01</span><div><h3>성장관리센터</h3><p>오늘 새롭게 발굴된 학생, 입시 상담이력, 다음 확인일을 한곳에서 관리합니다. 상담 원본은 학생정보 대시보드에 저장되고 이곳에서는 입시 유형만 활용합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${growthCenterMarkup()}</div>`;
  if(managementMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='inseoul'?'02':'03'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>내신과 모의고사 기준 충족 정도에 따라 핵심·성장·관심 관리군을 구성하고 이력을 지속 관리합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${admissionManagementMarkup(admissionMode==="inseoul"?"inseoul":"regional")}</div>`;
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='combined'?'04':admissionMode==='minimum'?'06':'·'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>학생별 내신·모의고사·수능최저·대학입결을 연결해 상담 근거를 확인합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>조회방식<select class="filter" id="admissionScope"><option value="individual">개인 학생 조회</option>${allowUniversityScope?'<option value="university">대학 기준 학생 찾기</option>':''}</select></label><label id="admissionClassLabel">반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="admissionStudentLabel">학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label>${needsUniversity?`<label id="admissionUniversityLabel">대학 선택<input class="search compact" id="admissionUniversity" list="admissionUniversityOptions" placeholder="대학명 입력 또는 선택"><datalist id="admissionUniversityOptions">${universities.map(name=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}"></option>`).join("")}</datalist><small>연결된 수능최저·입결 DB 기준 자동완성</small></label>`:""}<button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">${label} 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="admissionQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>◎</span><b>${label} 조회 대상을 선택하세요.</b><small>${allowUniversityScope?"개인 학생 또는 대학 기준 학생 찾기를 선택할 수 있습니다.":"학생을 선택하면 석차 기반 실제 9등급을 표시합니다."}</small></div></div></div>`;

  const bindLocalFilter = (inputSelector, rowSelector, resetSelector, extraFilter) => {
    const input = $(inputSelector);
    const reset = $(resetSelector);
    const run = () => {
      const query = (input?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      $$(rowSelector).forEach((row) => {
        const matchesText = !query || String(row.dataset.search || "").includes(query);
        const matchesExtra = extraFilter ? extraFilter(row) : true;
        row.classList.toggle("hidden", !(matchesText && matchesExtra));
      });
    };
    if (input) input.oninput = run;
    if (reset) reset.onclick = () => {
      if (input) input.value = "";
      run();
    };
    return run;
